Before each `web_extract` call, `require_current_evidence` reads the engine conformance evidence record for the connector (MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R807). It compares five fields: tenant, connector, deployed immutable revision, pack digest and both live tool fingerprints. The deployed revision and fingerprints come from fleet onboarding (`arbora/fleet/onboarding.py`). Missing, expired, revoked or mismatched evidence refuses `POLICY_DENIED` with reason `conformance_evidence_missing`, `conformance_evidence_expired`, `conformance_evidence_revoked` or `conformance_evidence_mismatch`. A refusal happens before any backend call. The check reads the engine and writes nothing. No operator flag grants admission. The handler commits only a `HarnessContrastDecision` record that names `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R803` and an adopted version reference. It forwards the record to the engine `DecisionCommit` method. The engine writes the adopted harness (`MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R802.1`). A human-scoped token is refused `PRINCIPAL_NOT_ALLOWED`. A record that does not name `R803` is refused `INVALID_ARGUMENT` with field `decision_kind`. The caller read check on the subject runs before the commit. `harness.evolution.adopt` binds to the `DecisionLog` op `HarnessAdoption` (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R803.8`). It carries the evaluated revision of `Definition { HarnessBundle, <agent_ref>:<task_class> }` (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.10`). Depends on: `ARBORA-OPS-R021.1`, `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R803.3`, `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R803.8`, `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.10`. | Test: `tests/api/test_harness_ops_served.py::test_adopt_refuses_human_token` (served). | `ARBORA-OPS-R700` | **Readiness snapshot joins independent probes.** `arbora/observability/readiness.py` exposes `collect_readiness_snapshot(probes, required, deadline_s, now)` returning the `arbora.readiness.v1` envelope. Probes run concurrently on a pool sized to the probe count; a probe that raises, overruns its deadline or returns an unknown state reports `unavailable`, never `ready`. A failed or missing required check (`engine`, `catalog`, `synthetic_query` by default) makes the snapshot `unavailable`; any other non-ready check makes it `degraded`. Identity is `ready` only for a session whose actor is authenticated. Every call re-probes; nothing is cached. `GET /dashboard/readiness` serves the snapshot from the engine probe and the probes added with `register_probe`. Budget: rollup under 1 ms for 16 probes, memory under 2 MiB beyond probe cost; wall time is bounded by the deadline. | `tests/observability/test_readiness.py` covers all-ready, each failure mode, optional degradation, missing required checks and the identity rule. |

| `ARBORA-OPS-R848.1` | **Source reads.** Serve `source.tree`, `source.blob`, `source.history` and `source.blame` as one typed read-only tool each. Each reads the engine's branch-aware source projection: one repository's `Branch` node, its live `FileVersion` memberships and their `Blob` content. Admission is per principal, with the exact engine scopes the read uses. Tree, history and blame use `node:read` and `edge:read`. Blob adds `blob:read`. Path admission uses the engine's row-level visibility under the caller's own claims. The stateless native reader composes at the serving root. An uncomposed reader fails closed with a typed `UNAVAILABLE`. The reader serves only what the substrate stores. Directories derive from the `FileVersion` path prefixes. The projection stores no directory nodes. `source.blame` reports the membership edge's per-file last touch. Its `line_level` is always `false`. `source.history` lists the last touch per live ref, without a commit DAG. Proving test: `tests/api/test_source_ops.py::test_source_reads_served` with `tests/mcp_server/test_source_reader.py`. | A served round trip returns the engine content under the caller's path ACL. A denied engine read propagates its engine code. A missing scope refuses with `SCOPE_REQUIRED` and an uncomposed reader returns `UNAVAILABLE`. |
| `ARBORA-OPS-R848.2` | **Source writes.** Subsystem: source operations. All three use `Effect.DESTRUCTIVE`, `Confirm.PLAN`, `Idempotency.KEY_REQUIRED` and `AuditClass.IDENTITY_CHAIN`. Scope `blob:write` and `node:write`. A call without an approved plan refuses `CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` with zero engine calls. A path without a write grant refuses `SUBJECT_ACCESS_DENIED`. Engine refusals pass through unchanged. The ops stay unregistered until the engine publishes `SubmitChangeset`. A registry test asserts they are absent before then. | `ARBORA-OPS-R849` | **Server-side landing policy.** Subsystem: source landing. Caller: served `invoke` (`arbora/api/invoke/pipeline.py::invoke`) → `source.land` → new `arbora/api/ops/source_landing.py::land_changeset`. Step 1: the handler materializes the changeset tree through the engine `Tree` read. Step 2: the `GateRunner` port runs the fast gates against that tree: format, hygiene, secrets, types and tests-fast. The default runner calls the repository-manager connector's gate tool through `fleet.call`. Each gate verdict writes to the engine as a `GateVerdict` record (`MEDULLA-SCM-R008`). Step 3: if all verdicts pass, the handler asks the engine to accept the changeset. A failing gate refuses `POLICY_DENIED` with reason `fast_gate_failed` and the gate id. Nothing lands. Step 4: after landing, the full suite runs asynchronously through the same port. On failure the engine records the bisect and revert decision (`MEDULLA-SCM-R009`). arbora keeps no merge queue and decides no revert. Config: `source.land.fast_gate_timeout_s` in `~/.config/arbora/config.yaml`, default 300, range 30 to 3600. A timeout counts as a failed gate. Depends on: `ARBORA-OPS-R848.2`, `MEDULLA-SCM-R008`, `MEDULLA-SCM-R009`. | Test: `tests/api/test_source_landing_served.py::test_fast_gates_run_before_landing_and_failure_has_zero_effect` (served). A post-landing failure records the engine bisect and revert decision. |

After approval, execution commits through `eg.coordination.DecisionLog` `learn` `MovePointer`, citing the committed `Promote` record. `revision.rollback` pins `expected_champion_ref` and calls `DecisionLog` `learn` `MovePointer` to the parent in the pointer stack. Arbora computes no score and no verdict. The approving operator becomes the pointer event principal (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.5`). | `ARBORA-OPS-R021.4.7` | **Durable, public run replay.** `runs.events` and `runs.get` use the admitted caller and canonical WorkItem owner before reading EG RunEvents. AU assigns stable event IDs and increasing per-run sequence. Arbora persists and projects them without creating events or inferring hidden thoughts. Bounded exclusive `after` cursors preserve gaps as unavailable. A bounded metadata census validates the full history before any payload page. Earlier gaps, duplicates and post-terminal events remain unavailable. Histories over the census bound remain unavailable too. Native owner and event-query refusals retain their engine codes on both transports. Public projections distinguish execution traces from explicit public summaries. They preserve source vocabulary and mark terminal events explicitly. Legacy HTTP reads invoke the same registry operation. Unknown, foreign, malformed and legacy unordered runs expose no event body. The operation carries the `RunTraceExportPage` of `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R009.100` unchanged. Arbora creates no events. | `tests/api/test_run_replay.py` proves owner-first reads, bounded cursor validation, terminal vocabulary, privacy allowlists and malformed/legacy/gap refusal. REST and MCP use the registered invoke path. |

## Shared execution explorer (specified)

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| `ARBORA-OPS-R021.4.8` | **Project an admitted execution graph.** Stage: served read operation `runs.execution_graph`. Caller: `invoke` → `handle_runs_execution_graph` → `NativeRunReader.execution_graph` → `arbora/api/ops/run_graph.py::project_graph`. Rule: keep replay semantics and decoder invariants. A new projection is versioned (`SCHEMA` in `run_replay.py`); an unsupported version refuses and never reinterprets event task ids. Return a bounded snapshot and ordered updates from the same owner-checked plan and `RunEvent` rows. Keep the root work item, the run and the run-local step as distinct identities. Node keys are `step_key(run_id, task_id)`. Edges are `spawned_child` or `depends_on`, never both implied. Dependencies never imply parentage. Only producer-declared lineage appears. The snapshot binds root, schema version, covered event cursor and completeness. Updates keep native event ids and sequence. Config: `runs_graph_max_nodes` default 500 and `runs_graph_max_edges` default 2000 in `~/.config/arbora/config.yml`, ranges [10, 5000] and [10, 20000]. Past a bound the snapshot is `partial`. Refusals: a gap, a conflicting identity or missing lineage returns an explicit `unavailable` or `partial` state. Arbora invents no edge. Writes: none. Depends on: R021.4.1, R021.4.2. | Test: `tests/api/test_execution_graph.py::test_snapshot_plus_updates_equals_fresh_snapshot` (L1) asserts equality at one cursor. Test: `tests/api/test_runs_served.py::test_fan_out_fan_in_reconnect_and_foreign_owner` (L3) covers repeated step names, bounded pages and foreign-owner refusal before any payload read. |
| `ARBORA-OPS-R021.4.9` | **Project permitted node records and capabilities.** Stage: served read operation `runs.node` beside `runs.execution_graph`. When indexing normalizes text, keep a separate NFC digest and the producer index digest, and validate the normalized-to-original byte map. Public offsets address the stored-text digest. | `ARBORA-OPS-R063.4` | **Retrieval evaluation refusals.** Stage: arbora served operations. Rule: denied scope refuses before dispatch. | `ARBORA-OPS-R070` | **Order-proposal confirmation operation and receipt.** Subsystem: finance approvals. Companion of `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R015.1`. Params: `proposal_id`, `admission_digest` (SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex) and `approver_set_digest`. The handler reads the engine admission receipt. It compares the digest and the checked actor. It then issues a confirmation receipt that binds those fields. It routes no order and calls no provider. Order routing stays in the emerald-exchange tools (EMERALD-OPS-R006). A stale or mismatched digest refuses `PLAN_STALE` with no receipt. The operation stays unregistered until the engine publishes the receipt type. Until then `get_registry` omits it and a registry test asserts the absence. Depends on: `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R015.1`, EMERALD-OPS-R006. | Test: `tests/api/test_finance_orders_served.py::test_confirmation_binds_digest_and_refuses_stale` (served). | `ARBORA-OPS-R071` | **Review-receipt producer for reviewed repository policy.** Subsystem: approvals. Companion of `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.7` and `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.8`. Operation `policy.review.approve` in new `arbora/api/ops/policy_review.py`, registered in the curated module loop of `arbora/api/ops/registry_factory.py::get_registry`. Caller: served `invoke` (`arbora/api/invoke/pipeline.py::invoke`) → `policy_review.py::handle_policy_review_approve`. `Confirm.PLAN`, `Idempotency.KEY_REQUIRED`, `AuditClass.IDENTITY_CHAIN`, scope `policy:review`. The receipt binds nine fields: repository, path, commit, blob digest, candidate-set digest and invariant generation. It also binds the expected active-policy revision, the reviewing actor and the receipt digest (SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex). The handler reads the current candidate set and invariant generation from the engine. A stale value refuses `PLAN_STALE`. Otherwise it returns the receipt. A rejected review returns no receipt. A source-file change uses governed write-back plus approval (`arbora/api/ops/write_back.py`). arbora holds no policy, grammar or activation authority. The engine validates the receipt. Depends on: `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.7`, `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.8`. | Test: `tests/api/test_policy_review_served.py::test_receipt_binds_every_field_and_refuses_stale` (served). A rejected review yields no receipt. |

Caller: served `invoke` (`arbora/api/invoke/pipeline.py::invoke`) → `maintenance.py::handle_paper_shortfall_run`. `Executor.SERVICE`, `Effect.DESTRUCTIVE`, `Confirm.PLAN`, `Idempotency.KEY_REQUIRED`, `AuditClass.IDENTITY_CHAIN`. Params: `owner_authorization_ref`, `expected_revision`, `cost_ceiling` (integer cents, 0 to 1,000,000) and `admission_verdict_ref`. The handler refuses before any effect in three cases. A missing authorization refuses `SCOPE_REQUIRED`. A stale revision refuses `PLAN_STALE`. A cost above the ceiling refuses `POLICY_DENIED` with reason `cost_over_ceiling`. It calls the engine maintenance method named in `MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R006.1` and returns the result receipt the engine reads back. It purges nothing that is leased, authoritative or the last source copy. The engine enforces this and arbora passes its refusal through. The dispatch target is the scholarx tool `paper_maintenance_apply` (`CONN-SCHOLARX-API-COVERAGE-R002`). The request carries the `PaperMaintenanceOrder` fields defined there. Depends on: `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R153`. | Test: `tests/api/test_retrieval_ops.py::test_listing_toc_digest_equals_read_span_digest` (served). It asserts digest equality and the pass-through refusal. |

A changeset from an agent principal (`MEDULLA-SCM-R012`) must cite `admission_record_digest`. `require_admission_record` reads the committed `Decide` record of kind `GeneratedCodeAdmission` (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R138.100`). It checks three things. The verdict is admit. The tenant equals the caller tenant. The artifact digest equals the digest of the proposed changeset content. Any other case refuses `POLICY_DENIED` with reason `admission_record_missing` or `admission_artifact_mismatch`, with zero engine writes. Arbora keeps materialization authority. It returns the sealed record digest and nothing else the engine owns. An absent producer refuses `UNAVAILABLE`. Each review result binds to the exact `base_rev` and `head_rev`. Each finding class needs evidence-backed findings. The declared low-impact filter applies. Depends on: `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R138.101`, `ARBORA-OPS-R848.3`. Refusals: `POLICY_DENIED` without a verified approval. | `ARBORA-OPS-R887` | **Served operation entity.approve_link.** Caller: the operation registry (`arbora/api/ops/registry_factory.py`, verify the current registry) → `arbora/api/ops/entity.py::approve_link` (new) → EG `Method::ApproveEntityLink { decision_record_digest, pair_index, approver }`. The operation is a destructive write with an approval step. It returns the engine receipt or the typed refusal unchanged. Refusals pass through: `LINK_NOT_APPROVABLE`, `LINK_APPROVER_MISSING`, `LINK_PROPOSAL_UNKNOWN`. The route accepts one run event or an array of at most `knob lineage.drain_batch` events. It maps the wire shape to `LineageEvent` and forwards under the caller's tenant. Arbora stores nothing and decides nothing. Refusals: 400 for a non-array body; 413 above the batch size; 403 without the scope. | `ARBORA-OPS-R891` | **Step usage events reach the usage ledger.** Subsystem: host bootstrap and run event sink. Caller: `arbora/agent_runs.py::record_run_event`, registered by `bind_run_ports` → `arbora/agent_host/usage_ledger.py::forward_step_usage`. Add `forward_step_usage` and `flush_usage(run_id)`. The sink buffers each `step_usage` event of MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R003.1 per run. At run end it sends the buffer as `Usage` `Append` batches (MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R801.8), each at most the engine `usage.max_batch`. The append goes through the service executor, because `usage:write` is service-only (MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R801.3). The tenant comes from the run context, never from an event field. Engine refusals pass through to the log; a refusal never fails the run and never drops the other batches. Depends on: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R801.8, MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R003.1. | Test: `tests/test_usage_forwarding.py::test_step_usage_events_append_once_per_run` (L3). A loopback engine records the principal, tenant and batches. It asserts the field set and the batch split. It also asserts that a refusal leaves the run result unchanged. |
